- Many files are missing. A list will be posted once the search is complete.

:::

:::info

- `MDC` refers to the Microsoft Download Center and is mentioned in headings only when files from multiple sources are listed.
- In **Windows Server 2008**, multi-edition files contain the Standard, Enterprise, and Datacenter editions.
- In **Windows Storage Server 2008**, multi-edition files contain the Standard, Enterprise, Workgroup and Basic editions.
- Some **clarifications** regarding **Windows Small Business Server 2008** files:
  - Windows Small Business Server 2008 is available in two editions: **Standard** and **Premium**.
  - The Standard edition includes only `Standard and Premium` files, whereas the Premium edition also includes `Component Repair`, `Additional Server`, and `SQL Server` files.
  - On MVS, only `Standard and Premium` and `Component Repair` files were released for both the initial release and Service Pack 2. On [MDC](https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/download/details.aspx?id=15237), all files were released, but not for Service Pack 2.
  - On MDC, Greek language files were also added.

:::
